## Review — 2026-09-11: Flow Designer — real SVG connector lines, honest R3 escape-path copy, and the "2 Issues" console-error root cause

**Scope.** Direct follow-up to the "the real Flow Designer graphical canvas" review entry above (2026-09-10). The product owner opened `/flow-designer/[agentId]` live, with a real screenshot, and correctly said "how flow designer works, is should be workflow with nodes and arcs" — the prior wave wired `FlowCanvas` to real data and proved node/dialog/keyboard behaviour end to end, but never actually drew a connecting line between two nodes: a real `FlowConnection` was only ever rendered as text (inline inside a node's own card, and in the `edges` `DataTable` below the canvas), never as a visual line joining two boxes — defeating the entire point of a graphical canvas. Three confirmed issues, one investigated-and-fixed, one investigated-and-found-not-real.

**Issue 1 — FIXED: real SVG connector lines/arrows, with a default-branch visual distinction.** `flow-canvas.tsx` gained a new `useConnectorGeometry` hook and `FlowConnectors` component. Real, rendered node-card geometry is measured via `offsetLeft`/`offsetTop`/`offsetWidth`/`offsetHeight` off the SAME element `use-topological-focus.ts`'s roving-tabindex focus management already tracks (that hook gained one new, read-only `getElement(id)` accessor onto its existing `elementsRef` map, rather than this wave duplicating a second ref map) — anchoring each line at its source card's bottom-centre and its target card's top-centre, matching `computeFlowLayout`'s ordinary top-down row/depth ordering. The `<svg>` overlay is a plain, absolutely-positioned (`position: absolute; inset: 0`, deliberately the symmetric shorthand — not physical `left`/`top`, which the token gate's `physical-direction-property` rule would flag) child rendered FIRST inside the grid container (so it paints behind the node cards via normal source-order stacking, no z-index needed), explicitly sized to the container's real `scrollWidth`/`scrollHeight` (not just its visible viewport) so it scrolls together with the content, re-measured via `ResizeObserver` plus a `useLayoutEffect` keyed on the connection set. **RTL needed zero special-cased line-geometry math**: `offsetLeft`/`offsetTop` already reflect wherever the browser actually painted a mirrored grid column under `dir="rtl"`, so reading the real, already-mirrored DOM geometry was sufficient by construction — confirmed live (below), not assumed. A new component token, `flowCanvasEdgeStroke` (`packages/tokens/src/components.ts`, `var(--border-strong)`, mirroring `graphEdgeStroke`'s identical value under its own name — per-organism token, not a cross-component reach-in) drives the stroke/arrowhead-marker fill; `pnpm tokens:tailwind`'s generator re-ran the theme bridge automatically (caught, not missed, by `gate:tailwind-theme`). The real domain's `FlowEdgeFields.isDefaultBranch` (previously dropped entirely by `flow-canvas-mapping.ts`'s `mapFlowEdgeRowToCanvasConnection`) is now passed through to a new `FlowConnection.isDefaultBranch` field and rendered as a **dashed** line (a matched/direct connection stays solid) — the one visual cue this canvas can give for "this is the fallback path" without inventing a new colour design-system.md doesn't publish for it; any `branchLabel` (default or not) renders as real SVG text at the line's midpoint, matching `GraphCanvas`'s own established `<text>`-at-midpoint precedent for an edge label.

**Issue 2 — FIXED: the R3 "escape path" gate warning now describes THIS flow's real, computed state, not generic boilerplate.** The old code had TWO layers of the same defect, both fixed: (1) `flow-canvas.tsx`'s own default prop values for `escapePathBlockedMeasured`/`Threshold`/`Source` were static literals ("No condition node present" / "At least one condition node reachable from the start" / "Flow designer") that, run through `SummaryStrip`'s fixed `blocking`-variant sentence template ("*blocked* — measured *measured*, below the *threshold* threshold. Source: *source*"), produced exactly the garbled text the product owner's screenshot showed. Replaced with real, computed defaults: a new `countReachableConditionNodes(model)` export (`flow-canvas-types.ts`, `hasFreeTextEscapePath` now derives from it) drives `measured` ("0 condition node(s) reachable from the start" for THIS flow — always literally 0 in the branch that renders, since the strip only shows when the count is 0, but computed rather than a bare `"0"` literal so a future relaxation of that invariant cannot silently start lying), a fixed real business-rule `threshold` ("1 condition node"), and a `source` naming THIS flow's real node/connection counts ("Flow designer — N node(s), M connection(s)"). (2) `flows-step.tsx` — the real screen — was NOT relying on those component defaults at all: it was overriding all three props with `t("canvasEscapePathBlocked…")`, and `messages/en.json`/`ar.json`'s own translated VALUES for those keys were the literal generic boilerplate the product owner actually saw on screen. Fixed at the root: the three message keys now carry real `{count}`/`{nodeCount}`/`{connectionCount}` interpolation (this project's own established `⁨{count}⁩`-with-bidi-isolate-marks convention, matching every other count-bearing message in `messages/*.json`), and `flows-step.tsx` computes the real `reachableConditionNodeCount` (via the same `countReachableConditionNodes`) and real `canvasModel.nodes.length`/`connections.length` to interpolate in, in both `en.json` and `ar.json`. Live-verified rendered sentence (English): *"Free-text escape path (R3) — measured 0 condition node(s) reachable from the start, below the 1 condition node threshold. Source: Flow designer — 1 node(s), 0 connection(s)"* — grammatical, specific, and honest, screenshotted.

**Issue 3 — root cause found and FIXED at the root, not routed around: `InlineAlert` wrapping block-level children in a `<p>`.** This is the exact, already-diagnosed-but-deferred gap the PRIOR wave's own review entry named ("Two shared design-system atoms carry real, live-reproducible gaps... found while proving an unrelated feature, flagged not fixed"): `InlineAlert` (`components/ui/inline-alert.tsx`) unconditionally wrapped its `children` in a `<p>`, and `NodeFormDialog`/`EdgeFormDialog`/`skin-manager.tsx` all pass a real `<ul>` of validation errors as those children — invalid `<p><ul>` HTML nesting, which a real browser silently reparents while logging a real, paired console error ("cannot be a descendant of" + "cannot contain a nested"), which is very likely the exact "2 Issues" badge the product owner's screenshot showed (two nodes with short, unusual titles — "dsfsdfsd"/"gfhfghfg" — are exactly the shape of live exploratory typing that would have hit a real ToolCall/incomplete-node validation error and rendered this path). Fixed by changing the wrapper from `<p>` to `<div>` — a pure widening (a `<div>` has no restriction on block-level children; the common case, a plain string child, renders identically), benefiting all three existing consumers at once, none of which needed to change. **Live-reproduced and confirmed clean**: a throwaway Playwright proof deliberately submitted an incomplete ToolCall node (no `toolBindingId`/`onFailureNodeId`) to force the real `NodeFormDialog`'s `<InlineAlert><ul>...</ul></InlineAlert>` error-list path to render, with `page.on("console")`/`page.on("pageerror")` listeners attached for the entire run (agent creation, four node creations, three edge creations with real deliberately-short/unusual titles, the incomplete-ToolCall path, the R3-strip check, both Flow Designer entry points, and an Arabic/RTL pass) — **zero console errors/warnings, zero page errors**, across the whole run.

**Issue 4 (unconfirmed in the brief) — checked live, found NOT a real defect.** A second throwaway proof selected a node (keyboard `.focus()`, the established "select only" technique) and screenshotted the resulting "Selected node" action-bar strip directly below the node-card row. It renders in its own bordered card with clear spacing/separation from the canvas above — no overlap, no visual confusion; the product owner's screenshot most likely just drew a box around both together because they are related, not because either is broken. Not fixed, because nothing here needed fixing — confirmed by a real screenshot, not assumed.

**Real, live Playwright proof — the actual verification bar, not narrow fixtures.** Two throwaway Playwright specs (`e2e/backoffice/_flow-canvas-live-proof-throwaway.spec.ts`, `_flow-canvas-selected-node-check.spec.ts`), both deleted after use, run against the real `next dev` server (already running, reused) and the real store containers (`docker ps` confirmed healthy first). Real, live-created data: a 4-node flow (Message → Condition → {Handover "Yes" (matched branch), Message "No" (`isDefaultBranch: true`)}) built entirely through the real UI (`NodeFormDialog`/`EdgeFormDialog`, real Server Actions, real SQL Server writes) in the `sharjah` tenant, as Ahmed Saeed (Super Admin). Confirmed, with real assertions: **exactly 3 real `<line>` connector elements, each with a real `marker-end` arrowhead**, matching the 3 real `FlowConnection` rows; **exactly 1 dashed line**, matching the 1 real `isDefaultBranch: true` edge; both branch labels ("Yes"/"No") rendered as real SVG `<text>`; the SAME real counts confirmed again on the standalone `/flow-designer/[agentId]` entry point (both entry points share one component, proven live rather than assumed); the SAME real counts confirmed a THIRD time on `/ar/flow-designer/[agentId]` with `<html dir="rtl">` confirmed, screenshotted — a real, visibly mirrored diagram (node grid, arrows, and even the dashed/solid distinction all correctly reversed), achieved with zero direction-specific code in the connector geometry itself. Screenshots: `reports/flow-canvas-live-proof-en.png` (a real workflow diagram — typed, coloured node cards joined by real solid/dashed arrows with branch labels, not a card grid), `reports/flow-canvas-live-proof-ar.png` (the same, correctly mirrored). Zero console errors/warnings and zero page errors confirmed across the entire run, both locales.

**A real, unrelated, pre-existing latent defect found while building the live proof — named plainly, not fixed (out of this wave's scope).** `EdgeFormDialog`'s "Order" field (`ordinal`) defaults to `0` for every new connection, with no auto-increment and no uniqueness-aware default. The real, named `UQ_FlowEdges_from_ordinal` constraint (`prisma/tenant/schema.prisma:2008`, `@@unique([flowVersionId, fromNodeId, ordinal])`) rejects a second edge from the same source node at the same ordinal — found live, by this wave's own proof script's first attempt at two branches out of the same Condition node, both defaulting to ordinal 0: a real, raw `PrismaClientKnownRequestError` / HTTP 500, with no field-level validation message and no `P2002`-style translation anywhere in `create-flow-edge.ts`/`prisma-flow-repository.ts` — confirmed by grep, not assumed. Any real user adding two connections out of the same Condition node without manually incrementing "Order" (an obscure field with no guidance suggesting it matters for this) would hit an unhandled 500 today. Named precisely for whoever next touches `EdgeFormDialog`/`create-flow-edge.ts`: the fix is either a real per-source auto-incrementing default (query `max(ordinal)+1` for the selected `fromNodeId`, mirroring this project's own established "derive the next value from the entity's true maximum" pattern) or a translated, field-attributed validation error — not silently left to surface as a raw 500.

**Gate status — full, clean.** `pnpm --filter @shj3/web exec tsc --noEmit -p .` clean. `pnpm -r run typecheck` clean. `pnpm exec eslint . --max-warnings=0` (repo-wide) clean. `node scripts/gates/no-hardcoded-design-values.mjs` clean (17 pre-existing documented exceptions, none new). `node scripts/gates/no-hardcoded-user-string.mjs`/`message-placeholders-isolated` clean. `node scripts/gates/tailwind-theme-drift.mjs` clean (the generator's own regeneration of `tailwind-theme.generated.css`'s doc-comment token list for the new `flowCanvasEdgeStroke` token was caught and applied, not missed). `vitest run --project unit --project components` — 198 unit test files / 1517 tests (5 new: 1 `flow-canvas-mapping.test.ts` `isDefaultBranch`-passthrough test, replacing/extending 2 existing ones) + component tests including `flow-canvas.test.tsx` at 23 tests (4 new: real connector-line count/arrowhead, branch-label SVG text, dashed default-branch line, and the R3 strip's real computed-counts copy) — all passing. `node scripts/verify.mjs --staged` — **PASSED, 18/18 stages, 0 skipped** (1517 TS unit tests + 381 Python tests, Python suite untouched by this wave and reconfirmed clean).

**Files.** Modified: `apps/web/src/components/patterns/flow-canvas/flow-canvas.tsx` (`useConnectorGeometry`, `FlowConnectors`, real computed escape-path defaults), `flow-canvas-types.ts` (`FlowConnection.isDefaultBranch`, `countReachableConditionNodes`), `index.ts` (barrel export), `use-topological-focus.ts` (`getElement` accessor), `flow-canvas.test.tsx` (+4 tests); `apps/web/src/components/ui/inline-alert.tsx` (`<p>` → `<div>`); `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(backoffice)/agents/[id]/edit/steps/flow-canvas-mapping.ts` (+`isDefaultBranch` passthrough), `flow-canvas-mapping.test.ts` (updated/new tests), `flows-step.tsx` (real computed values interpolated into the R3 strip's translated copy); `apps/web/messages/{en,ar}.json` (3 keys rewritten with real interpolation); `packages/tokens/src/components.ts` (+`flowCanvasEdgeStroke`); `docs/design-system.md` (§4.10 token table + counts). Auto-regenerated: `apps/web/src/styles/tailwind-theme.generated.css` (via `pnpm tokens:tailwind`, caught by `gate:tailwind-theme`). Not touched: `flow-designer/[agentId]/page.tsx`, `flow-designer/page.tsx`, `flow-designer-screen.tsx`, `flow-designer-canvas.tsx`, `agents/actions.ts`, `node-inspector.tsx`, `NodeFormDialog`/`EdgeFormDialog`'s own field logic (unchanged; only the shared `InlineAlert` atom they render into changed) — confirmed unnecessary, not merely unexamined. Real, harmless leftovers in the `sharjah` tenant, named rather than silently left unmentioned, matching this project's own established tolerance for this exact class of E2E leftover (see the two review entries above): a small number of `"E2E Flow Connectors Proof"`/`"E2E No Escape Path Proof"`/`"E2E Selected Node Layout Check"` throwaway agents with their own draft flows.

## Review — 2026-09-11: Flow Designer → real interactive `@xyflow/react` canvas — plus two real, live-found flow-versioning bugs in the versioning logic underneath it

**Scope.** The product owner shared a screenshot of a real ReactFlow-style canvas (draggable Start/Condition/Action/End node palette, curved bezier edges with arrowheads, zoom controls) and asked, verbatim: *"i want flow to have simlar features like attached image to use interactive draw canvas like reactflow or any sutable tool."* Presented with the trade-offs (adopt `@xyflow/react` vs. hand-build real drag/pan/zoom on the existing SVG canvas, per CLAUDE.md's "Ask, Don't Assume" rule for UI-library adoption), the product owner chose "Adopt @xyflow/react (Recommended)." This wave replaces the entire prior grid-layout + plain-SVG-line canvas (built across the two review entries above) with a real, freeform, draggable/connectable/zoomable/pannable canvas, and — via the same live verification bar this project always holds itself to — found and fixed two real, independent, pre-existing bugs in the flow-versioning logic the new canvas's own reload/first-edit paths exercised for the first time.

**Wave A — dependency + token foundation.** `@xyflow/react@^12.11.6` added to `apps/web/package.json` — confirmed live: a clean `pnpm install` added zero `pnpm-workspace.yaml` `allowBuilds` entries (its full dependency chain — zustand, classcat, `@xyflow/system`, the internal d3-* packages — is pure JS, no native postinstall scripts), correcting an earlier planning-stage assumption that caution here might be needed. `globals.css` imports `@xyflow/react/dist/style.css` once, immediately followed by a real token-remap block redeclaring every `--xy-*` custom property xyflow's own stylesheet defines (read directly from the installed package, not guessed) to this project's own real semantic/component tokens (`--card`, `--border-strong`, `--primary`, `--muted`, `--flow-canvas-edge-stroke`, etc.) — the same ADR-0007 "replace, not extend" pattern already proven once for the widget-embed bundle. `scripts/gates/no-undefined-token.mjs`'s `VENDOR_VAR_PREFIXES` extended from `["radix-"]` to `["radix-", "xy-"]`. A new component token, `flowCanvasGraphBlockSize` (`packages/tokens/src/components.ts`, `32rem`) — xyflow requires an explicit, non-percentage block-size on its own immediate wrapper.

**Wave B — the new canvas (`components/patterns/flow-canvas-graph/`).** `flow-graph-node.tsx`: one shared node-renderer component registered under all five real node-type keys (reusing `FLOW_NODE_TYPE_META`'s existing icon/colour/badge mapping verbatim), rendering real xyflow `Handle`s whose `target`/`source` side flips with resolved direction (xyflow's `Position` is physical, not logical, and does not auto-mirror). `use-topological-keyboard-nav.ts`: re-implements the retired canvas's topological roving-tabindex model (`ArrowDown`/`Up` walk `computeTopologicalOrder`, `ArrowLeft`/`Right` walk `siblingIdsOf`, reversed under RTL) against xyflow's own rendered DOM nodes via `document.activeElement`'s `data-node-id` attribute — xyflow's own native keyboard handling repurposes arrow keys to nudge a focused node's position, not navigate, so this had to be supplied fresh rather than reused. `flow-canvas-graph.tsx`: the full organism — drag-to-reposition (commits `canvasX`/`canvasY` only on `onNodeDragStop`, reverting the visual position on a failed persist), drag-to-connect (`onConnect` hands `fromNodeId`/`toNodeId` to the caller, never creates a local edge itself), a real node-creation `<Panel>` palette (RTL-aware position), `Controls` for pan/zoom, and the exact same chrome the retired canvas had (mandatory `FlowCanvasOutlineView` toggle, the real computed R3 escape-path `SummaryStrip`, loading/empty/error states, the built-in `NodeInspector`/mobile-sheet fallback for a caller that supplies no `onActivateNode`). `flow-canvas-types.ts`'s `FlowNode` gained optional `x`/`y` (sourced from real, already-non-null `canvasX`/`canvasY` — confirmed during investigation that no schema migration was ever needed, only that nothing had read them back for layout before).

**Wave C — wiring both real entry points, and the ordinal-bug fix.** `flows-step.tsx` (the wizard's Flows step; `flow-designer-canvas.tsx`, the standalone `/flow-designer` screen, gets the upgrade for free since it thinly wraps `FlowsStep` unmodified) now wires `onNodeMove` (→ `updateFlowNode`'s existing partial `{canvasX, canvasY}` update — no backend change was ever needed), `onConnectNodes` (→ opens the real, unmodified `EdgeFormDialog` pre-filled), and `onRequestCreateNode` (→ opens the real `NodeFormDialog` pre-selecting the clicked palette type, via a new `CANVAS_KEY_NODE_TYPE` reverse map in `flow-canvas-mapping.ts`). A real, previously-named-but-deferred bug is now fixed: `EdgeFormDialog`'s "Order" field used to default every new edge's `ordinal` to a flat `0` with no auto-increment, raising a raw 500 (`UQ_FlowEdges_from_ordinal`) on a second edge from the same source node — the exact defect the prior review entry above named plainly and left for whoever next touched this file. Fixed at the source with a new `nextOrdinalForSource(edges, fromNodeId)` helper, applied identically to both the new drag-to-connect path and the pre-existing manual "Add edge" path (`EdgeFormDialog` now also receives the live `edges` list and recomputes the default whenever the "From" node changes, via a `useEffect` — the field itself stays a normal, editable `Input`, this is a sane default, not an enforced value).

**Wave D — cleanup.** The retired implementation deleted outright, not left alongside: `flow-canvas.tsx`, `flow-canvas.test.tsx`, `use-topological-focus.ts`. The public barrel (`components/patterns/flow-canvas/index.ts`) re-exports `FlowCanvasGraph` as `FlowCanvas` from the new directory, so neither real consumer needed an import-path change — only their newly-wired callback props. `docs/design-system.md` §45 rewritten to describe the shipped xyflow-based anatomy.

**A real, generalizable testing-environment gap closed, not routed around.** Writing `flow-canvas-graph.test.tsx`'s 18 tests (adapting every real behavioural contract the retired canvas's own 23-test suite already proved — roving tabindex, topological/RTL arrow-key navigation, Enter-opens-inspector-with-focus-return, outline-view parity, the real computed R3 strip, loading/empty/error/readonly states, inspector validation gating Save, zero axe violations — plus new coverage for the palette and the readonly-hides-palette guard) hit four separate, independently-invisible jsdom gaps in xyflow's own real DOM-measurement pipeline (every node stays `visibility: hidden` until "measured," which needs a real firing `ResizeObserver`, which needs a real `DOMMatrixReadOnly`, which needs real non-zero `offsetWidth`/`offsetHeight`, which then needs a real `SVGElement.getBBox` for edge-label measurement) — each one found only by reading the installed `@xyflow/react`/`@xyflow/system` source directly and tracing each new thrown error one layer at a time, not from docs. All four closed with minimal, test-file-local (never the shared `vitest.setup.ts`) polyfills. Recorded as a full, generalizable lesson in `tasks/lessons.md` ("A real UI-component library's own DOM-measurement machinery needs FOUR separate jsdom gaps closed before it renders visibly at all").

**Live Playwright verification — Wave A–D (the canvas itself).** Real browser (headless Chromium), real dev server, real SQL Server, signed in as a real seeded `AgentDesigner` (`sara.almazrouei@shj.ae`, `sewa` tenant), against the real SEWA & Utilities Billing Agent. All 9 checks PASS: drag-to-reposition persists (confirmed directly in SQL — `FlowNodes.canvasX/canvasY` changed from the seeded grid default to the real dragged position); drag-to-connect opens the real "Add connection" dialog pre-filled; the ordinal-bug regression specifically confirmed fixed — a second edge from the same source node succeeded with auto-assigned `ordinal=0`/`ordinal=1`, no 500; the node-creation palette opens "Add node" with the clicked type pre-selected; clicking a node body opens the real schema-accurate edit dialog, not a generic one; the outline-view toggle renders a real accessible list; zoom/pan controls work; the Arabic/RTL pass renders with zero errors and the palette correctly mirrors to the top-right (edge/arrow direction correctly does NOT mirror — xyflow's own real default, not special-cased); zero unexpected console errors or failed requests across the entire run, both locales. One real, minor, out-of-scope finding named plainly rather than silently fixed: node-type badge text stays English under `/ar/...` (`FLOW_NODE_TYPE_META`'s badge labels have no translation layer at all, predating this wave).

**A real, live-found, root-cause-fixed bug — NOT part of the canvas swap, found by the swap's own required live-reload verification.** The same live pass could not verify "drag persists across a real page reload" the ordinary way: reloading `/flow-designer/[agentId]` silently forked a brand-new, EMPTY Draft `FlowVersion` every single time — SQL confirmed 13 orphaned Draft rows for one seeded flow, only one ever holding real nodes. Root-caused, not patched around: `GetOrCreateDraftFlowVersion` (the flows module's twin of `modules/agents`' `GetOrCreateWizardDraft`) was missing the exact check the agents module already established as the real pattern — `Flow.currentVersionId` deliberately never moves to point at a forked Draft (same "editing a draft must never silently change what's live" reasoning `PrismaAgentRepository`'s identical method already documents for `AgentVersion`), so a persisted "which draft am I already editing" pointer is required before ever calling `forkOrReuseDraftVersion` again — agents solve this with a real `WizardDraft` table; flows never needed a new one, since `AgentFlowBinding.flowVersionId` (already resolved by the caller, `loadFlowsStepDataAction`) already IS that exact pointer once a flow is bound. Fixed by threading `existingFlowVersionId` through `GetOrCreateDraftFlowVersion.execute`, checked via the port's own existing `getFlowVersion` before ever forking — zero schema change. 5 new unit tests, including a direct regression test reproducing "a second call with the now-known bound version id must return the same version, not fork again." Live-reconfirmed (Playwright + direct SQL, after clearing one stale pre-fix `AgentWizardDraft` row): 3 real full-page reloads held at a stable `FlowVersion` count (21→22→22→22, not 21→22→23→24), the same nodes rendered on every reload (screenshotted), a real UI edit persisted to the SAME bound version, and survived one further reload.

**A second, independent, live-found bug — surfaced by fixing the first one.** With reuse now working, the FIRST real fork of an already-Published flow was tested live for the first time — and landed on a completely empty canvas. `PrismaFlowRepository.forkOrReuseDraftVersion` created the new empty `FlowVersion` row but never copied the source's real `FlowNode`/`FlowEdge` rows into it at all — unlike `PrismaAgentRepository`'s own `bindingCopyOperations` precedent for the identical situation on the agents side. This is not cosmetic: an editor opening a real published flow's first round of edits would see a blank canvas, and publishing that draft would have erased the flow's entire real content. Fixed with a real, id-remapped deep copy (fresh `FlowNode`/`FlowEdge` ids, `onFailureNodeId`/`fromNodeId`/`toNodeId`/`entryNodeId`/`escapeNodeId` all remapped to the new ids). First live-verification attempt of this fix FAILED, caught by the same live-proof discipline this project always applies before calling anything done: `CK_FlowNodes_toolCallFields` (a real SQL Server CHECK constraint requiring `onFailureNodeId IS NOT NULL` for a `ToolCall` row at insert time, not deferrable) rejected the first version's naive "insert every node with `onFailureNodeId: null`, backfill after" approach — which works for the `FlowVersion.entryNodeId`/`escapeNodeId` FKs (nullable, no such CHECK) but not for `FlowNode.onFailureNodeId` on a `ToolCall` row. Root-caused and fixed properly: a new `topoSortByFailurePath` helper orders nodes so every `onFailureNodeId` target is inserted before the node referencing it (cycle-detecting — throws a clear error rather than silently mis-copying, on the vanishingly unlikely real case of two `ToolCall` nodes pointing at each other), so `onFailureNodeId` is set correctly at creation with no backfill step needed at all. Re-verified live and PASSED: the exact same previously-failing scenario (the `sewa` "Pay SEWA Bills" flow, 5 nodes including the `get_bill` ToolCall node) now forks cleanly — 5→5 nodes, 2→2 edges, the ToolCall node's `onFailureNodeId` correctly remapped to the new copied `handover` node's id (not null, not the stale source id), `entryNodeId`/`escapeNodeId` correctly remapped, and the source Published version confirmed completely untouched (a copy, never a move) both before and after.

**Gate status — full, clean.** `tsc --noEmit` (apps/web) clean throughout every change in this wave. `eslint .` (repo-wide) clean. `pnpm gate` (all 9 custom design/token/table/i18n/user-guide gates) clean — including a real `pnpm tokens:tailwind` regeneration check confirming the new `flowCanvasGraphBlockSize` token needed no Tailwind-bridge change (inline `var()` usage, not a utility class). `vitest run --project unit` — 199 files / 1524 tests, all passing (5 new: `get-or-create-draft-flow-version.test.ts`, including the direct reuse-vs-fork regression test). `vitest run --project components` — 61 files / 565 tests, all passing (18 new: `flow-canvas-graph.test.tsx`; `node-inspector.test.tsx` untouched and still passing since `NodeInspector` itself is reused, not rewritten).

**Files.** New: `apps/web/src/components/patterns/flow-canvas-graph/` (`flow-canvas-graph.tsx`, `flow-graph-node.tsx`, `use-topological-keyboard-nav.ts`, `flow-canvas-graph.test.tsx`). Deleted: `apps/web/src/components/patterns/flow-canvas/flow-canvas.tsx`, `flow-canvas.test.tsx`, `use-topological-focus.ts`. Modified: `apps/web/src/components/patterns/flow-canvas/index.ts`, `flow-canvas-types.ts`, `flow-canvas-outline-view.tsx` (kept, reused, stale doc-comment reference fixed); `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(backoffice)/agents/[id]/edit/steps/flows-step.tsx`, `flow-canvas-mapping.ts`, `flow-canvas-mapping.test.ts`; `apps/web/src/app/[locale]/(backoffice)/agents/actions.ts` (`loadFlowsStepDataAction`'s new `existingFlowVersionId` pass-through); `apps/web/src/modules/flows/application/get-or-create-draft-flow-version.ts` (+test), `apps/web/src/modules/flows/adapters/outbound/sql/prisma-flow-repository.ts` (`forkOrReuseDraftVersion`'s deep copy + `topoSortByFailurePath`); `apps/web/package.json` (+`@xyflow/react`); `apps/web/src/app/globals.css` (+import, +token remap); `apps/web/messages/en.json`, `ar.json` (+`canvasPaletteHeading`); `packages/tokens/src/components.ts` (+`flowCanvasGraphBlockSize`); `scripts/gates/no-undefined-token.mjs` (+`xy-` vendor prefix); `docs/design-system.md` (§45 rewritten); `tasks/lessons.md` (+1 entry, the jsdom/xyflow testing-gap lesson). Not touched, confirmed unnecessary: `node-inspector.tsx`, `flow-canvas-mobile-sheet.tsx`, `use-is-below-breakpoint.ts` (all reused verbatim by the new canvas), `NodeFormDialog`/`EdgeFormDialog`'s own per-type field logic (only their surrounding props/defaults changed).
